437. ✅ **2026-06-16 - DEEP-AUDIT cycle 40: DSV4 Spear of Shojin Focused Will ability-amp valuation (commit `c5fe019d`) - Tier-2 ENGINE 1.126.0 -> 1.127.0, DS :8893 restarted -> 1.127.0, Share re-synced (340 files) in the SAME commit.** Operator-directed continuation of the DSV-* P6-G5 / G2-residual scorer-valuation lane (the A1-UIX3 plan set was DONE, so this slice was director-refilled from the SAME residual the operator seeded DSV1/2/3 against; the P6-G2 work-map bucket B names Spear of Shojin explicitly). **ROOT CAUSE:** the ability scorers had NO seam for Spear of Shojin 3161 Focused Will, a stacking ability/passive damage amp (Meraki 16.12.1: "3% per stack ... max 4 stacks" = 12%); the item carried `defensive_only=True` with the self-documenting note "ability damage not DPS-modeled" (`_effects_data.py:653`), so an ability-reliant build that bought Shojin saw ZERO damage value from it - only its raw AD/AH stats. Focused Will amps ABILITIES and PASSIVES only, never basic attacks (the generic `damage_amp_pct` batch-14 field was deliberately left off Shojin since it would amp AAs too). The exact "AD scorer-valuation" residual class from ledger 425 bucket B (Hubris/Collector did DSV2, lethality did DSV3, Shojin is the ability-amp sibling). **FIX (DEFAULT-OFF `assume_ability_amp` seam, byte-identical when off; INLINE sole orchestrator - one coupled contract across 6 engine files; R9 + the DSV1/2/3 precedent):** (1) `_effects_types.ItemEffect` gains `ability_damage_amp_per_stack` / `ability_damage_amp_max_stacks` appended at the END (dataclass convention), default 0.0 / 0 = byte-identical. (2) `effects.py` NEW `total_ability_damage_amp(effects, stacks)` = `sum(per_stack * min(stacks, max_stacks))`, 0.0 for a no-Shojin build (mirrors `total_takedown_bonus_ad`). 433. ✅ **2026-06-16 - DEEP-AUDIT cycle 36: DSV3 lethality-vs-sustained-AD burst-ranker valuation (commit `27873cc1`) - Tier-2 ENGINE 1.125.0 -> 1.126.0, DS :8893 restarted -> 1.126.0, Share re-synced (339 files) in the SAME commit.** Fifth session of the operator-directed plan refill (P6-G5 residual 3, the last DSV-* slice). **ROOT CAUSE:** `rank_items_by_burst` defaulted `target_armor=0.0`, and against zero armor `effects.effective_target_armor` floors its penetration tail at zero (`max(0.0, armor - pen_flat)`), so lethality (flat armor pen, Riot V14.1 1:1) contributed NOTHING to a ranked item's `delta_burst` - an equal-cost raw-AD item out-ranked a lethality item. This is the exact "lethality-vs-sustained tradeoff in `burst`" residual flagged by P6-G5 (ledger 424): a burst assassin's real target is a squishy CARRY with armor, against which flat pen bites; the engine's static ranker assumed a 0-armor dummy. The existing `test_lethality_helps_zed` proves `compute_burst_damage` values lethality CORRECTLY at `target_armor=80` - the gap was only in the RANKER's default target. **FIX (DEFAULT-OFF `assume_squishy_target` seam, byte-identical when off; INLINE sole orchestrator per directive - one coupled engine contract, R9 + DSV1/DSV2 precedent):** (1) `burst.py` NEW module consts `_SQUISHY_TARGET_BASE_ARMOR=22.0` / `_SQUISHY_TARGET_ARMOR_PER_LEVEL=4.5` + `_assumed_squishy_target_armor(level)` = a representative mage/ADC armor curve (22 base + 4.5 per level above 1 -> 44.5 @ L6, 67 @ L11, 98.5 @ L18; uses the engine's `clamp_level` which VALIDATES, not clamps). (2) `rank_items_by_burst` gains `assume_squishy_target: bool = False` appended at the END (dataclass/signature convention) - when True AND `target_armor <= 0.0`, a local `ranking_target_armor` is substituted into BOTH the baseline and EVERY candidate `compute_burst_damage` call (and the `BurstRankResult.target_armor` for honest surfacing) + a note; when False OR the caller pinned `target_armor>0`, `ranking_target_armor == target_armor` so the ranking is BYTE-IDENTICAL. The lethality math itself is untouched (`effective_target_armor`, ENGINE 1.5.1 V14.1 1:1) - only the ranker's target ASSUMPTION is refined. **SEAM-FIRST scope (per directive + EXCLUDED):** ships DEFAULT-OFF; the live `rank.py` scorers do NOT pass `assume_squishy_target=True` yet - flipping it ON is a separate real-game-validation-gated step (the DS Phase-D default-ON flag-flip class), so live rankings are byte-identical this cycle. **TDD:** `test_burst_squishy_target_dsv3.py` (7) red-first (ImportError on `_assumed_squishy_target_armor`) -> green: armor-curve positive/monotonic/anchor-points (22/67/98.5) + out-of-range raises (clamp_level contract); seam OFF == default == today's armor=0 ranking; seam ON sets `result.target_armor` to the assumed 67 (OFF=0); **the lethality item (Youmuu's 3142) out-values the raw-AD item (Bloodthirster 3072) MORE under the seam** via a DIFFERENCE-OF-DIFFERENCES assertion `(on_leth - on_ad) > (off_leth - off_ad)` (robust, not a fragile absolute cross-item rank - per the Testing Discipline "prefer computed quantities" rule); an explicit `target_armor=80` is NOT overridden (seam no-op). **Gate (Tier-2 dual suite):** DS-dir **7126 passed / 1 skip / 1942 subtests, exit 0**; tests/ green after the DS restart + `ds_share_sync` (the 7 pre-fix failures = 1 live-:8893 stale-version integration [`phase8_smoke` build_profile] + 6 Share doc/ingest anchors, ALL 18 green on re-run); ruff + py_compile clean; `ds_share_sync --check` in sync (339 files, engine 1.126.0). ENGINE bump touched 75 quoted-assertion pins across 67 files + the sustain-contract docstring; no historical changelog refs touched (`feedback_engine_bump_quoted_literal_only`). **Verifier subagent GATE RUN per the directive's explicit mandate (overrides R7): ALL 7 CONFIRM** (test file exists, 7 passed, ENGINE literal 1.126.0, 0 stale pins, --check in sync, full DS suite green, 18/18 drift files) - GATE PASS, 0 regressions. No frozen files touched. **NEXT (plan order): UIX1/2/3** (5-phase champ-select-SR / Home+Settings / Session+detached-PGR UI audits) - the DSV-* P6-G5 scorer-valuation residual is now fully DONE (DSV1 AP DoT-burn + DSV2 kill-state passives + DSV3 lethality-vs-sustained). Source: `docs/ORCHESTRATION_PLAN.md`.

432. ✅ **2026-06-15 - DEEP-AUDIT cycle 35: DSV2 takedown / kill-state item-passive valuation (commit `f1075c38`) - Tier-2 ENGINE 1.124.0 -> 1.125.0, DS :8893 restarted -> 1.125.0, Share re-synced (338 files) in the SAME commit.** Fourth session of the operator-directed plan refill (P6-G5 residual 2). **GAP (P6-G5, ledger 424):** the burst + auto scorers had NO seam for the on-takedown item passives, so the kill-state lethality items were under-ranked (Hubris ~#13, The Collector ~#12 live) - flagged as the "item passives needing kill/takedown state" residual. **FIX (DEFAULT-OFF kill-state OFFENSE seam, byte-identical when off; INLINE sole orchestrator - the seam is one coupled contract across 6 engine files; R9 + DSV1/OVL2 precedent):** (1) `_effects_types.ItemEffect` gains 3 fields appended at the END (dataclass convention) - `takedown_bonus_ad_base` / `takedown_bonus_ad_per_stack` (Hubris Eminence "15 (+2 per stack)" bonus AD on takedown, verbatim Meraki 16.12.1) + `execute_max_hp_pct` (The Collector Death "execute below 5% of maximum health"); all default 0.0 = byte-identical. (2) `effects.py` NEW `total_takedown_bonus_ad(effects, stacks)` + `total_execute_max_hp_pct(effects)` aggregators. (3) `dps.py` NEW module const `_ASSUMED_TAKEDOWN_STACKS=1` (operator-tunable, mirrors `ehp._TAKEDOWN_RATE_PER_FIGHT`) + an `assume_takedown` kwarg on `compute_dps` - when True folds Hubris bonus AD (15 + 2*1 = 17) into the rotation AD (`stats_for_rotation["ad"]`) + `CallContext.bonus_ad` + the per-hit display AD so AA DPS + bonus-AD procs rise; The Collector execute is DELIBERATELY NOT credited (a one-shot finisher is not sustained DPS). (4) `burst.py` `assume_takedown` on `compute_burst_damage` + `rank_items_by_burst` - threads into the internal `compute_dps` AA probe (AA per-hit picks up Hubris AD) AND the `AbilityContext.bonus_ad` (AD-ratio abilities scale off it, so Hubris raises BOTH ability and AA), and after the rune layer credits the Collector execute as a `5% * target_max_hp` TRUE-damage finisher (`execute_finisher_damage`, folded into `total_burst_damage`, needs a target max HP to size). NEW `BurstResult.takedown_bonus_ad` + `execute_finisher_damage` fields mirror the `rune_proc_damage` convention (0.0 default = byte-identical) + to_dict + notes. **KEY DESIGN GUARD (root-cause-honest):** Death's Dance carries NEITHER offense field - its takedown payoff is the Defy HEAL, already valued on the SURVIVABILITY axis (`ehp.py ItemHeal.takedown_gated`, ENGINE 1.57.0); crediting it offense damage would double-count phantom value, so the seam is a deliberate no-op for DD (a test pins DD offense byte-identical seam-on vs seam-off). **Data wired:** Hubris 6697/226697/126697 + The Collector 6676/667666/226676. **TDD:** `test_dsv2_killstate_passives.py` (16) red-first (ImportError on `_ASSUMED_TAKEDOWN_STACKS`) -> green: schema defaults, Meraki data pins (Hubris 15/2, Collector 0.05, DD none), helper math (17 AD @ 1 stack), OFF byte-identical (dps + burst + rank), Hubris DPS+burst raise (ability AND AA), Collector execute == 100 vs 2000 max HP folded into total, execute needs target HP, DD offense byte-identical. **SEAM-FIRST scope (per directive + EXCLUDED):** ships DEFAULT-OFF; the live rank scorers (`rank.py`) do NOT pass `assume_takedown=True` yet - flipping it ON is a separate real-game-validation-gated step (the DS Phase-D default-ON flag-flip class), so live rankings are byte-identical this cycle. **Gate (Tier-2 dual suite):** DS-dir **7119 passed / 1 skip / 1942 subtests, exit 0**; tests/ **7982 passed / 2 skip / 109 subtests** (the 7 pre-fix failures = 1 live-:8893 stale-version integration + 6 Share doc/ingest anchors, ALL green after the DS restart + `ds_share_sync`, re-run confirmed 18/18); ruff + py_compile clean; `ds_share_sync --check` in sync (338 files, engine 1.125.0). Verifier subagent SKIPPED per R7 (single-thread inline edit, no stale pipe) - fresh re-verify done instead. ROOT CAUSE: on 2026-06-03 the operator hand-edited the gist README directly on the remote (2 commits `a76cd91`+`8129ed5`, README-only), putting the remote +2 ahead of the local `~/.rc-share-gist` clone; every subsequent hook `git push origin HEAD` hit `! [rejected] (non-fast-forward)`, `_git` runs `check=True` so it raised CalledProcessError, and the git post-commit context SWALLOWED it - 45 regenerated commits accrued unpushed over 6 days with zero surfaced signal (the local mirror was correct the whole time; only the publish was blocked). FIX 1 (ops, operator-gated fork - chose "force-push lean template now" over "port the richer hand-authored README into `_README_TEMPLATE`" or "hold push"): `git push --force origin HEAD:main` -> published gist now `17ba6ab` ENGINE 1.120.0 / patch 16.12.1, fresh Share.zip (346 files), divergence cleared (`## main...origin/main`, in sync). Dropped the 2 manual-README-edit commits (reflog-recoverable; the sync regenerates README from `_README_TEMPLATE` by design, so the manual edits were always transient). FIX 2 (prevent recurrence, TDD, commit `e1fc7603`): `tools/gist_share_sync.py` (frozen=no per its arch header) - NEW `_do_push()` catches the push CalledProcessError -> `_append_log()` (git stderr + unpushed `origin/main..HEAD` count -> `logs/YYYY-MM-DD.log`) + `_write_status()` (atomic `ops/runtime/gist_sync_status.json`: `ok`/`detail`/`unpushed_commits`/`ts`) + stderr echo + returns non-zero `PUSH_FAIL_EXIT=3` (a post-commit hook can't abort the commit, but the failure is now LOGGED + health-signalled + non-zero-exited instead of silent). The no-change path also computes the unpushed backlog now -> a latent backlog is surfaced (status `ok=False` + non-zero exit) even when `Share/` is unchanged, so a false "up to date" can no longer mask a stale publish. +3 regression tests `tests/test_gist_share_sync.py` (failure path: status+log+stderr+exit code; success path: ok=True/unpushed 0; `_unpushed_count` parse + git-error safety). VERIFY (ground truth this turn): RED 3-fail -> GREEN 3-pass; ruff All checks passed; py_compile OK; hygiene trio (smart-quote/mojibake/u2500) green; live smoke `python tools/gist_share_sync.py` exit 0 + `gist_sync_status.json` = `{ok:true, detail:"up to date", unpushed_commits:0}`; zero production importers (only the post-commit hook runs it as a script, so no blast radius). **Don't-redo:** do NOT hand-edit the gist REMOTE (it diverges the remote AND the sync clobbers it from `_README_TEMPLATE` on the next push); the gist staleness health signal is `ops/runtime/gist_sync_status.json` (gitignored; `unpushed_commits`>0 or `ok=false` == stale mirror) - a future dashboard/rc_facts surface can read it. 357. ✅ **2026-06-08 - REPLAY1 operator-reported "match ingestion is not up to date after each game" - ROOT-CAUSE fix + data recovery; non-frozen `scripts/rewind_catchup.py`, ENGINE untouched 1.120.0, no Share, BACKEND+DATA only (no UI), gitignored data files not committed (gemini-headless-upgrade loop, operator "start next open items", commit `647b455e`).** Replay / rewind surface froze at 2026-05-23 while the operator kept playing. ROOT CAUSE (verified live via Riot Account-V1 + Match-V5 probes): `resolve_current_puuid` derived the account from the DB MAJORITY puuid (`operator_riot_id_from_db` -> most-frequent puuid's Riot ID -> Account-V1). After the operator switched Riot ID (SamplePlayer#Vayne -> #Trist), the DB stayed dominated by ~2900 OLD-account rows, so resolution kept re-locking the old account + never discovered the new one (chicken-and-egg: the new account has 0 DB rows yet). `lib/rewind_live_writer.py` read the stale puuid from `data/rewind_catchup.state.json` + only ever saw already-ingested old matches -> every 90s post-gameEnd live write was a silent `already_present` no-op. Probes: state puuid -> old Vayne (old URF matches, ALL already in DB); `get_account_by_riot_id("SamplePlayer","Trist")` -> the current puuid with 37 un-ingested recent matches. FIX: a persisted `riot_id` in the state sentinel is an authoritative STICKY override - `resolve_current_puuid` resolves it via Account-V1 BEFORE the DB-majority fallback (new priority step 3, ahead of step 4 DB-derived); `main()` persists `args.riot_id`. A freshly-switched account can ONLY be discovered from an explicit/persisted identity, never the majority. DATA RECOVERY (same fix, per data-fix discipline): ran `catchup --riot-id "SamplePlayer#Trist"` -> state.json corrected (puuid=rO-hF + riot_id), 37 matches backfilled, rewind DB newest 2026-05-23 -> 2026-06-08 (2904 -> 2941 rows); live writer now resolves the current account (`_resolve_latest_puuid` -> rO-hF, verified). TDD `tests/test_rewind_catchup_puuid.py` (7) pins the priority order (persisted beats DB-majority, explicit flags beat persisted, Account-V1-miss / malformed-id degrade to DB-derived). RC suite 5376 passed / 2 skip / 0 fail; ruff clean. LIVE end-to-end (next-game auto-ingest) OWED. **Don't-redo:** the DB-majority resolution was the bug - do NOT revert step 3; a future DIFFERENT-account switch needs one `catchup --riot-id NAME#TAG` run (or wiring the live LCU current-summoner Riot ID into the writer, logged FUTURE) before auto-ingest resumes. (session record `docs/ORCHESTRATION_PLAN.md` Findings 2026-06-08)

351. ✅ **2026-06-08 - parallel next-items: vision :8889 false-alarm probe fix + cdragon hard-tail CLOSED - non-frozen; ENGINE untouched 1.120.0, no Share, no DS restart, no RC restart (rc_facts is a SessionStart-hook diagnostic + ROADMAP/BACKLOG docs only).** Operator "start the next open items in parallel" -> 2 independent parallel tracks, both ground-truthed + resolved. **Track A - vision :8889 = FALSE ALARM + probe fix (`7f49499d`).** Session-start rc_facts flagged "Legion: vision server :8889 not listening" (RC-VisionServer task Running). Verify-first DISPROVED it: the server is UP - PID 2108 `moon_vision_server.py` bound `0.0.0.0:8889`, public `/health` HTTP 200 (`{alive:true, model:claude-haiku-4-5, api_key_ok:true, uptime ~6.3h}`), netsh portproxy empty. Root cause = the PROBE: `tools/rc_facts.py::_port_listening` did ONE 0.4s TCP connect and read any failure as down; the single-threaded vision server's accept() loop stalls intermittently mid frame-upload, so a fast connect times out while the port is provably LISTENING (live 5x socket probe: 3 OK at 0-15ms + 2 timeouts at ~405ms). A refused connection (truly down) raises immediately, NOT a timeout - so a timeout must NOT be read as down. Fix: `_port_listening` now retries on `socket.timeout` (3 attempts x 1.0s) and returns False immediately ONLY on a non-timeout OSError (refused). +5 tests `tests/test_rc_facts_port_probe.py` (real-listener True, closed-port False, timeout-then-success retry, all-timeouts False, refused-no-retry; a timing-assert variant was dropped - a closed Windows loopback port TIMES OUT rather than refusing, so "fast refused" is not assertable cross-platform, covered instead by the deterministic monkeypatch refused-no-retry test). Spawned follow-up chip `task_a647378a` = make the :8889 server threaded (the accept-stall ROOT cause), GATED on first confirming its handlers make no blocking inline model calls (a model client exists -> threading could allow concurrent Sonnet calls -> cost/RPM risk). **Track B - cdragon Phase-2 ratio hard-tail (NEXT-UP #1) = CLOSED (`d2ecf125`; scope + by-level drift-check, NO engine change).** A 2-agent scope+drift pass over the live 16.11.1 sidecar (`data/daemon_slayer/16.11.1/cdragon_ability_ratios.json`, 171 champs re-fetched, totals match committed `_n_fallback_blocks:577`): the 577 Meraki-fallback ratio blocks bucket as 292 emission-guard tooltip aggregates (MaxDamageTooltip / Cast2DamageMax / TotalShield - must STAY fallback by design, "fixing" them injects aggregate/duplicate damage) + ~174 needing live target/stack/branch state (buff-counter 26 / conditional-gate 1 / unknown-stat-enum 47 / EffectValue+StatBySubPart unmodeled / resource+CDR-scaled) = CLOSED-as-Meraki, the SAME live-state class as the operator-CLOSED item-232 conditional-target-state arc + 111 by-level (per-champ-level arrays: ByCharLevelBreakpoints linear+stepped / ByCharLevelInterpolation / bare Breakpoint). Only the by-level bucket is offline-resolvable, so it was drift-checked vs the frozen Meraki the engine consumes (`champion_abilities.json damage_blocks`): VERDICT ALIGNED, not stale. Decisive finding - cdragon by-level indexes by CHAMP LEVEL 1..18 while Meraki indexes by SPELL RANK 1..5 (different axes of the same growth, which is WHY the per-rank Meraki schema cannot represent them and the resolver falls them back); the 107 evaluable blocks are overwhelmingly auxiliary (caps / minion+monster mods / heal+shield / durations / move-speed / resist-grants / modifiers whose primary damage is already a sibling MECHANICAL calc the engine reads), and the genuine same-quantity pairs AGREE (Zeri Q 17.06 vs 17.14 = 0.5%, Vladimir Q 110 vs 111 = 0.9%, AurelionSol E 150 vs 150 = 0.0%, Pantheon Q 123.5 vs 115 = 7.4%; the apparent "65/80 drift >10%" was spurious cross-mechanic pairing - flat caps / 0-modifiers / heals / durations mis-paired to a damage _pct). Re-sourcing would need a NEW champ-level-indexed schema axis (not a value swap) for near-zero gain = churn -> by-level DEFERRED to BACKLOG (only if a future surface needs a champ-level-indexed ratio axis). Operator chose drift-check-first from a framed 3-way (build-now / close-all / drift-check) -> the data drove DEFER. **Verify:** local gate GREEN this turn - `ruff check .` all-passed, py_compile (rc_facts + test) OK, hygiene trio + 5 probe tests = 17 passed; CI success on BOTH d2ecf125 + 7f49499d (gh run 27115133400 / 27113939131). Scratch evidence (census + drift rows + probe scripts) under `docs/_scratch/` (gitignored). **Don't-redo:** cdragon hard-tail is CLOSED - do NOT re-pitch a calc-graph resolver (conditional/buff-counter/target-HP = live-state, Meraki-permanent; by-level = aligned-not-stale, a different axis not a stale value); remaining cdragon GROWTH needs a NEW extractor key, not a resolver (memory `reference_ds_forward_marker_exhausted`). 350. ✅ **2026-06-07 - RC-GeminiAudit nightly task fix (`tools/gemini_audit.ps1`) - exit-code-masking bug + diagnostics + model fallback; non-frozen; no engine / Share / DS / RC touch (the script + its outputs are gitignored advisory state).** Session-start anomaly flagged `RC-GeminiAudit` LastTaskResult=1 (the nightly gemini read-only auditor). Root-caused 2 layers + verified the external cause live. **(1) Code bug (FIXED):** the runner used `Write-Error "..."; exit N` for its 2 failure paths (missing key -> 2; gemini empty after retries -> 3), but the surrounding `$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'` makes `Write-Error` TERMINATING - it threw a WriteErrorException BEFORE reaching `exit N`, so PowerShell exited a bare **1** with NOTHING logged (the gemini_audit.log success-write is the last line, never reached on failure). The task showed an undiagnosable exit 1 (no log line for the 06-07 03:00 run). Replaced both sites with a NEW `Fail($msg,$code)` helper: appends a `FAIL code=N <reason>` line to `logs/gemini_audit.log` + `Write-Warning` (never terminating, WarningPreference not ErrorActionPreference) + `exit $code` - so failures now carry the INTENDED code + a logged reason. **(2) Resilience (ADDED):** new `Invoke-GeminiAudit($model,$tries)` helper + a model FALLBACK - the primary `RC_GEMINI_MODEL` (operator-set `gemini-3-pro-preview`, which WORKED through 06-06) is tried 3x; on persistent empty it falls back to `gemini-2.5-flash` (override via `RC_GEMINI_FALLBACK_MODEL`) for 2 more tries before Fail(); the output header + log now record the model ACTUALLY used (`$usedModel`), so a preview-model-specific throttle no longer kills the advisory audit. **(3) External root cause (verified live, NOT code-fixable):** the key is VALID - `GET v1beta/models?key=` lists 50 models - but `generateContent` returns **429 Too Many Requests** for `gemini-2.5-flash` (quota / RPM exhausted; the memory notes free-tier quota is shared with Google Antigravity). The 429 self-recovers on quota reset; my back-to-back repro/probe calls (~10 in 20 min) also added RPM load the once-nightly cadence does not. **Verify:** PowerShell `Parser::ParseFile` clean + 0 non-ASCII bytes (CLAUDE.md PS-double-quoted-string mojibake guard); a live background run exercised + logged BOTH the fallback and the Fail() path (exit 3); the audit outputs (`docs/EXTERNAL_REVIEW_*.md` + `ops/runtime/gemini_last_audit.txt` marker + `logs/gemini_audit.log`) are all gitignored -> the ONLY tracked change is `tools/gemini_audit.ps1`. Deliberately did NOT re-run the scheduled task (it would re-hit the same 429 -> exit 3; it goes green on the next cadence once quota frees - do not hammer it). Memory `project_gemini_auditor` updated. **Don't-redo:** the exit-code masking is FIXED - a future nonzero RC-GeminiAudit now logs WHY to `logs/gemini_audit.log` (read that FAIL line FIRST). A genuine CS->game flip still promotes via the ungated GameStart/InProgress phase arms, so no real promotion is lost. +5 tests (`ChampSelectNullBlipLiveGateTests`, RED->GREEN); view-router 38 passed, mode-flap-dom 12 passed; node --check + py_compile clean. Applied behavior UNCHANGED (display-only).
